## Complete Frontend Projects Page Exploration Report

I've thoroughly explored the frontend implementation for the projects page. Here's the complete breakdown:

### 1. MAIN PROJECTS PAGE COMPONENT

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx` (748 lines)

This is a client-side component (`"use client"`) that serves as the main projects listing page. Key features:

- **View Modes:** Toggle between "cards" (grid) and "compact" (list) views via `LayoutGrid`/`List` icons
- **Search:** Real-time filtering by project name (resets to page 1)
- **Pagination:** Client-side pagination with 12 items per page (PAGE_SIZE = 12)
- **Data Fetching:** Uses SWR to fetch up to 100 projects: `projectsApi.list(1, 100)`
- **Create Dialog:** Modal for creating new projects with optional source
- **View Mode Toggle:** Located in header with search and create button

**Key Code Snippet:**
```typescript
const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
const filtered = search
  ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
  : items;
```

---

### 2. PROJECT-RELATED COMPONENTS

#### **A. ProjectCard Component** (Lines 410-449)
Three-column layout card showing:
- **Header:** Project logo + name + description with animated arrow on hover
- **Sources Section:** List of configured sources with provider icons, status dots (green=active), and poll intervals
- **Recent Releases Section:** Last 5 releases with version badges, provider icon, repository name, and "View all" link
- **Semantic Releases Section:** Last 3 semantic releases with urgency color badges

```typescript
function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border">
      {/* Header */}
      {/* Content columns: sources | recent releases | semantic releases */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 border-t">
        <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
        <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
        <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### **B. ProjectCompactRow Component** (Lines 453-564)
Single-row layout showing:
- **Project Name** (left, 160px min-width): Logo + name
- **Latest Release** (140px width): Version badge + provider icon, clickable link
- **Latest Semantic Release** (flex-1): Version badge + urgency badge + truncated summary (60 chars max)
- **Navigation Arrow** (right, gray)

```typescript
function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
  const latest = releases[0];
  const latestSr = srItems[0];
  return (
    <div onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}>
      {/* Project name */}
      {/* Latest release */}
      {/* Latest semantic release */}
      {/* Arrow */}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### **C. SourcesSection Component** (Lines 152-230)
Displays three mini-sections:
- **Section Header:** "Sources" label + "Add Source" button
- **Source List:** Each source shows:
  - Provider icon (13px)
  - Repository name (monospace, 12px)
  - Status indicator (green dot if enabled, gray if disabled) 
  - Status text "Active"/"Disabled"
  - Poll interval (formatted, e.g., "1d", "6h")
- **Inline Source Form:** Modal-like form below list for quick add

**Hosts the `InlineSourceForm`** (Lines 23-148):
- Takes projectId, shows provider select
- Validates repository format
- Includes filters (version include regex, exclude regex)
- Exclude pre-releases checkbox (GitHub only)
- Calls `sourcesApi.create()` and `sourcesApi.poll()`

#### **D. RecentReleasesSection Component** (Lines 234-304)
- Fetches last 5 releases per project
- Shows version badge (gray bg, monospace) + provider icon + repository name + timeAgo
- Links to `/releases/{id}`
- "View all" link to `/releases?project={projectId}`

#### **E. SemanticReleasesSection Component** (Lines 315-397)
- Fetches last 3 semantic releases
- Shows version badge (blue bg) + status text + urgency badge + truncated summary
- Urgency colors: critical (red), high (orange), medium (amber), low (gray)
- Links to `/projects/{id}/semantic-releases/{srId}`
- "View all" link to `/semantic-releases?project={projectId}`

**Urgency Color Map:**
```typescript
const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
  medium: { bg: "#f59e0b", text: "#ffffff" },
  low: { bg: "#6b7280", text: "#ffffff" },
};
```

#### **F. ProjectCardLogo Component** (Lines 401-406)
Wrapper around `ProjectLogo` that fetches sources for the project and passes them for avatar rendering.

### 3. PROJECT DETAIL PAGE

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/projects/project-detail.tsx` (872 lines)

Full project management interface with tabs:

**Tabs:**
1. **Sources** - Table with provider badge, repository, interval, toggle, last polled, edit/delete actions
2. **Context Sources** - Table with type badge, name, config URL, delete action
3. **Semantic Release Settings** - Agent config, trigger rules, custom prompt, test run interface

**Features:**
- Inline editable project name and description (click to edit, click-outside to save)
- Source count and context source count displayed in header
- Delete project button
- Test run interface (select source → select version → run)
- Run history table with status, duration, links to semantic releases

### 4. API HOOKS & DATA FETCHING

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`

**Projects API:**
```typescript
export const projects = {
  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) => 
    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  get: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),
  create: (input: ProjectInput) => request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {...}),
  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) => request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {...}),
  delete: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, {...}),
};
```

**Sources API:**
```typescript
export const sources = {
  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1) => 
    request<ApiResponse<Source[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/sources?page=${page}`),
  create: (projectId: string, input: SourceInput) => {...},
  get: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<Source>>(`/sources/${id}`),
  update: (id: string, input: SourceInput) => {...},
  delete: (id: string) => {...},
  poll: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<{ new_releases: number }>>(...),
};
```

**Releases API:**
```typescript
export const releases = {
  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Release[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/releases?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  listBySource: (sourceId: string, page = 1) => {...},
  get: (id: string) => {...},
};
```

**Semantic Releases API:**

export const semanticReleases = {
  list: (projectId: string, page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    request<ApiResponse<SemanticRelease[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  listAll: (page = 1, perPage = 25) => {...},
};

### 5. DATA SHAPES (TypeScript Types)

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`

**Project:**
```typescript
interface Project {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  agent_prompt?: string;
  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}
```

**Source:**
```typescript
interface Source {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  provider: string;  // "github" | "dockerhub" | "ecr-public" | "gitlab"
  repository: string;  // "owner/repo" format
  poll_interval_seconds: number;
  enabled: boolean;
  version_filter_include?: string;  // regex
  version_filter_exclude?: string;  // regex
  exclude_prereleases?: boolean;
  last_polled_at?: string;
  last_error?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}
```

**Release:**
```typescript
interface Release {
  id: string;
  source_id: string;
  version: string;
  raw_data?: Record<string, unknown>;
  released_at?: string;
  created_at: string;
  project_id?: string;
  project_name?: string;
  provider?: string;
  repository?: string;
}
```

**SemanticRelease:**
```typescript
interface SemanticRelease {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  project_name?: string;
  version: string;
  report?: SemanticReport;
  status: string;  // "pending" | "completed" | "failed"
  error?: string;
  created_at: string;
  completed_at?: string;
}

interface SemanticReport {
  subject?: string;
  urgency?: string;  // "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low"
  summary?: string;
  recommendation?: string;
  risk_level?: string;
  availability?: string;
  adoption?: string;
  // ... more fields
}
```

### 6. DESIGN SYSTEM & COMMON COMPONENTS

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/ui/`

**Buttons:** Standard shadcn button with variants (default, destructive, outline, secondary, ghost, link)

**Badges:** 
- `ProviderBadge` - Shows provider icon + label with branded colors:
  - GitHub: #1a1a1a (black)
  - Docker Hub: #2496ed (blue)
  - ECR Public: #ff9900 (orange)
  - GitLab: #fc6d26 (orange)
- `UrgencyChip` - Colored badges for urgency levels
- `VersionChip` - Gray badge with monospace text for versions

**Cards:** Shadcn card components with header, title, content, footer

**Forms:** Input, Label, Textarea, Select components

**Dialogs:** Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle from shadcn

**Other:**
- `ProjectLogo` - Avatar with provider icons (supports GitHub, GitLab avatars)
- `StatusDot` - Colored indicator dots for statuses
- `ConfirmDialog` - Confirmation modal for destructive actions
- `SectionLabel` - Styled h3 for section headings

**Font System:**
- `--font-fraunces` - Display font (h1, titles) 
- `--font-dm-sans` - Body font (text, UI labels)
- `'JetBrains Mono'` - Monospace (version numbers, repository names, code)

**Color Palette:**
- Primary accent: `#e8601a` (orange)
- Text: `#111113` (near-black), `#374151` (dark gray), `#6b7280` (medium gray), `#9ca3af` (light gray)
- Borders: `#e8e8e5` (light beige)
- Backgrounds: `#ffffff` (white), `#fafaf9` (off-white), `#f3f3f1` (light gray)

### 7. PAGINATION IMPLEMENTATION

**Projects Page Pagination (Client-Side):**
```typescript
const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
const paged = filtered.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);

{totalPages > 1 && (
  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
    <button onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}>Previous</button>
    <span>Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}</span>
    <button onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}>Next</button>
  </div>
)}
```

**Semantic Releases Page (Server-Side Meta):**
```typescript
const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;
const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
// Shows: {startRow}–{endRow} of {total}
```

---

### 8. KEY FEATURES SUMMARY

| Feature | Implementation |
|---------|-----------------|
| **Search** | Client-side string matching on `project.name` |
| **View Toggle** | Icon buttons switching between card/compact layouts |
| **Pagination** | 12 items per page, client-side slicing, Previous/Next buttons |
| **SWR Caching** | Named cache keys: `project-{id}-card-sources`, `project-{id}-card-releases` |
| **Inline Forms** | Add sources directly within card without modal |
| **Empty States** | "No projects yet" message with create button |
| **Loading States** | "Loading..." text while fetching |
| **Badges/Chips** | Provider-branded icons, urgency colors, version formatting |
| **Time Display** | `timeAgo()` utility: "2h ago", "5d ago", etc. |
| **Responsive Grid** | `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3` for 3-column card sections |

    23→function InlineSourceForm({
    24→  projectId,
    25→  onDone,
    26→}: {
    27→  projectId: string;
    28→  onDone: () => void;
    29→}) {
    30→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState("github");
    31→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState("");
    32→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState("86400");
    33→  const [versionFilterInclude, setVersionFilterInclude] = useState("");
    34→  const [versionFilterExclude, setVersionFilterExclude] = useState("");
    35→  const [excludePrereleases, setExcludePrereleases] = useState(false);
    36→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    37→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    38→
    39→  const handleAdd = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    40→    e.preventDefault();
    41→    if (!repository.trim()) return;
    42→    setSaving(true);
    43→    setError("");
    44→    try {
    45→      const repoError = validateRepository(provider, repository.trim());
    46→      if (repoError) {
    47→        setError(repoError);
    48→        setSaving(false);
    49→        return;
    50→      }
    51→      const res = await sourcesApi.create(projectId, {
    52→        provider,
    53→        repository: repository.trim(),
    54→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval) || 86400,
    55→        enabled: true,
    56→        version_filter_include: versionFilterInclude.trim() || undefined,
    57→        version_filter_exclude: versionFilterExclude.trim() || undefined,
    58→        exclude_prereleases: excludePrereleases || undefined,
    59→      });
    60→      if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
    61→      mutate(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`);
    62→      onDone();
    63→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    64→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to add source");
    65→    } finally {
    66→      setSaving(false);
    67→    }
    68→  };
    69→
    70→  return (
    71→    <form onSubmit={handleAdd} className="mt-2 rounded-md border p-3 space-y-2" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
    72→      {error && <div className="text-[12px] text-red-600">{error}</div>}
    73→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    74→        <select
    75→          value={provider}
    76→          onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
    77→          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"

    91→        />
    92→        <input
    93→          type="number"
    94→          min={60}
    95→          value={pollInterval}
    96→          onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)}
    97→          className="w-20 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
    98→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
    99→          title="Poll interval (seconds)"
   100→        />
   101→      </div>
   102→      <input
   103→        type="text"
   104→        value={versionFilterInclude}
   105→        onChange={(e) => setVersionFilterInclude(e.target.value)}
   106→        placeholder="Include filter (regex, optional)"
   107→        className="w-full rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"
   108→        style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   109→      />
   110→      <input
   111→        type="text"
   112→        value={versionFilterExclude}
   113→        onChange={(e) => setVersionFilterExclude(e.target.value)}
   114→        placeholder="Exclude filter (regex, optional)"

   252→      >

   256→      {releases.length > 0 ? (

   308→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
   309→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
   310→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
   311→  medium: { bg: "#f59e0b", text: "#ffffff" },
   312→  low: { bg: "#6b7280", text: "#ffffff" },
   313→};
   314→
   315→function SemanticReleasesSection({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
   316→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sr`, () =>
   317→    srApi.list(projectId, 1, 3)
   318→  );
   319→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   320→
   321→  return (
   322→    <div className="border-t md:border-t-0 px-4 py-3" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   323→      <span
   324→        className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mb-1 block"
   325→        style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   326→      >
   327→        Semantic Releases
   328→      </span>
   329→
   330→      {items.length > 0 ? (
   331→        <div className="space-y-1">
   332→          {items.map((sr) => {
   333→            const urgencyStyle = sr.report?.urgency
   334→              ? URGENCY_COLORS[sr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   335→              : undefined;
   336→            return (
   337→              <Link
   338→                key={sr.id}
   339→                href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   340→                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] rounded px-1 -mx-1 py-0.5"
   341→              >
   342→                <span
   343→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none"
   344→                  style={{
   345→                    backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   346→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   347→                    color: "#1d4ed8",
   348→                  }}
   349→                >
   350→                  {sr.version}
   351→                </span>
   352→                <span
   353→                  className="text-[11px]"
   354→                  style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   355→                >
   356→                  {sr.status}
   357→                </span>
   358→                {urgencyStyle && (
   359→                  <span
   360→                    className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none"
   361→                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   362→                  >
   363→                    {sr.report!.urgency}
   364→                  </span>
   365→                )}
   366→                {sr.report?.summary && (
   367→                  <span
   368→                    className="text-[11px] truncate flex-1"
   369→                    style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}
   370→                  >
   371→                    {sr.report.summary.length > 60
   372→                      ? sr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   373→                      : sr.report.summary}
   374→                  </span>
   375→                )}
   376→                <span className="ml-auto text-[11px] shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   377→                  {timeAgo(sr.completed_at || sr.created_at)}
   378→                </span>
   379→              </Link>
   380→            );
   381→          })}
   382→          <Link
   383→            href={`/semantic-releases?project=${projectId}`}
   384→            className="block text-[11px] mt-1 hover:underline"
   385→            style={{ color: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   386→          >
   387→            View all &rarr;
   388→          </Link>
   389→        </div>
   390→      ) : (
   391→        <p className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   392→          No semantic releases yet
   393→        </p>
   394→      )}
   395→    </div>
   396→  );
   397→}
   398→
   399→/* ---------- Project Card Logo ---------- */
   400→
   401→function ProjectCardLogo({ projectId, name }: { projectId: string; name: string }) {
   402→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
   403→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
   404→  );
   405→  return <ProjectLogo name={name} sources={data?.data} size={40} />;
   406→}
   407→
   408→/* ---------- Project Card ---------- */
   409→
   410→function ProjectCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   411→  return (
   412→    <div
   413→      className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   414→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   415→    >
   416→      {/* Header */}
   417→      <div className="px-4 py-3">
   418→        <div className="min-w-0 flex items-center gap-3">
   419→          <ProjectCardLogo projectId={project.id} name={project.name} />
   420→          <div className="min-w-0">
   421→            <Link
   422→              href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   423→              className="group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[16px] font-bold transition-colors"
   424→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a" }}
   425→            >
   426→              {project.name}
   427→              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 opacity-0 -translate-x-1 transition-all group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0" />
   428→            </Link>
   429→            {project.description && (
   430→              <p
   431→                className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] truncate"
   432→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   433→              >
   434→                {project.description}
   435→              </p>
   436→            )}
   437→          </div>
   438→        </div>
   439→      </div>
   440→
   441→      {/* Content columns: sources | recent releases | semantic releases */}
   442→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   443→        <SourcesSection projectId={project.id} />
   444→        <RecentReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   445→        <SemanticReleasesSection projectId={project.id} />
   446→      </div>
   447→    </div>
   448→  );
   449→}
   450→
   451→/* ---------- Compact Row ---------- */
   452→
   453→function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   454→  const router = useRouter();
   455→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-releases`, () =>
   456→    releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 5)
   457→  );
   458→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sources`, () =>
   459→    sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id)
   460→  );
   461→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sr`, () =>
   462→    srApi.list(project.id, 1, 3)
   463→  );
   464→
   465→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   466→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   467→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   468→  const latest = releases[0];
   469→  const latestSr = srItems[0];
   470→
   471→  const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
   472→  for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
   473→
   474→  const latestSrc = latest ? sourceMap.get(latest.source_id) : undefined;
   475→  const latestIcon = latestSrc ? getProviderIcon(latestSrc.provider) : undefined;
   476→
   477→  const urgencyStyle = latestSr?.report?.urgency
   478→    ? URGENCY_COLORS[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   479→    : undefined;
   480→
   481→  return (
   482→    <div
   483→      onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}
   484→      className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#f9f9f7] cursor-pointer"
   485→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   486→    >
   487→      {/* Project name */}
   488→      <span
   489→        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-bold shrink-0"
   490→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a", minWidth: "160px" }}
   491→      >
   492→        <ProjectLogo name={project.name} sources={sources} size={24} />
   493→        {project.name}
   494→      </span>
   495→
   496→      {/* Latest release */}
   497→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ minWidth: "140px" }}>
   498→        {latest ? (
   499→          <>
   500→            <Link
   501→              href={`/releases/${latest.id}`}
   502→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   503→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-300 transition-shadow"
   504→              style={{
   505→                backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   506→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   507→                color: "#374151",
   508→              }}
   509→            >
   510→              {latest.version}
   511→            </Link>
   512→            {latestIcon && latestIcon({ size: 12, className: "shrink-0", style: { color: "#9ca3af" } })}
   513→          </>
   514→        ) : (
   515→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   516→            No releases
   517→          </span>
   518→        )}
   519→      </span>
   520→
   521→      {/* Latest semantic release */}
   522→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   523→        {latestSr ? (
   524→          <>
   525→            <Link
   526→              href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   527→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   528→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none shrink-0 hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   529→              style={{
   530→                backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   531→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   532→                color: "#1d4ed8",
   533→              }}
   534→            >
   535→              {latestSr.version}
   536→            </Link>
   537→            {urgencyStyle && (
   538→              <span
   539→                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none shrink-0"
   540→                style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   541→              >
   542→                {latestSr.report?.urgency}
   543→              </span>
   544→            )}
   545→            {latestSr.report?.summary && (
   546→              <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   547→                {latestSr.report.summary.length > 60
   548→                  ? latestSr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   549→                  : latestSr.report.summary}
   550→              </span>
   551→            )}
   552→          </>
   553→        ) : (
   554→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   555→            No analysis
   556→          </span>
   557→        )}
   558→      </span>
   559→
   560→      {/* Arrow */}
   561→      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }} />
   562→    </div>
   563→  );
   564→}
   565→
   566→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   567→
   568→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   569→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   570→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
   571→  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   572→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
   573→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   574→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   575→
   576→  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
   577→  const filtered = search
   578→    ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
   579→    : items;
   580→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   581→  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
   582→  const paged = filtered.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);
   583→
   584→  return (
   585→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   586→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   587→        <div>
   588→          <h1
   589→            style={{
   590→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   591→              fontSize: "24px",
   592→              fontWeight: 700,
   593→              color: "#111113",
   594→            }}
   595→          >
   596→            Projects
   597→          </h1>
   598→          <p
   599→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   600→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   601→          >
   602→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   603→          </p>
   604→        </div>
   605→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   606→          <div className="relative">
   607→            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   608→            <input
   609→              type="text"
   610→              value={search}
   611→              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
   612→              placeholder="Search projects..."
   613→              className="rounded-md border py-1.5 pl-8 pr-3 text-[13px] w-48"
   614→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   615→            />
   616→          </div>
   617→          <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   618→            <button
   619→              onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
   620→              className="p-1.5 rounded-l-md transition-colors"
   621→              style={{
   622→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "cards" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   623→                color: viewMode === "cards" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   624→              }}
   625→              title="Card view"
   626→            >
   627→              <LayoutGrid className="h-4 w-4" />
   628→            </button>
   629→            <button
   630→              onClick={() => setViewMode("compact")}
   631→              className="p-1.5 rounded-r-md transition-colors"
   632→              style={{
   633→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "compact" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   634→                color: viewMode === "compact" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   635→              }}
   636→              title="Compact view"
   637→            >
   638→              <List className="h-4 w-4" />
   639→            </button>
   640→          </div>
   641→          <button
   642→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   643→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   644→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   645→          >
   646→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   647→            New Project
   648→          </button>
   649→        </div>
   650→      </div>
   651→
   652→      {isLoading ? (
   653→        <p
   654→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   655→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   656→        >
   657→          Loading...
   658→        </p>
   659→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   660→        <div
   661→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   662→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   663→        >
   664→          <p
   665→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   666→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   667→          >
   668→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   669→          </p>
   670→          <button
   671→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   672→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   673→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   674→          >
   675→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   676→            New Project
   677→          </button>
   678→        </div>
   679→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   680→        <p
   681→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   682→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   683→        >
   684→          No projects matching &ldquo;{search}&rdquo;
   685→        </p>
   686→      ) : (
   687→        <>
   688→          <div className={viewMode === "cards" ? "space-y-4" : "space-y-1.5"}>
   689→            {paged.map((project) =>
   690→              viewMode === "cards" ? (
   691→                <ProjectCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   692→              ) : (
   693→                <ProjectCompactRow key={project.id} project={project} />
   694→              )
   695→            )}
   696→          </div>
   697→          {totalPages > 1 && (
   698→            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
   699→              <button
   700→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
   701→                disabled={currentPage <= 1}
   702→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   703→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   704→              >
   705→                Previous
   706→              </button>
   707→              <span
   708→                className="text-[13px]"
   709→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   710→              >
   711→                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
   712→              </span>
   713→              <button
   714→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
   715→                disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
   716→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   717→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   718→              >
   719→                Next
   720→              </button>
   721→            </div>
   722→          )}
   723→        </>
   724→      )}
   725→
   726→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   727→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   728→          <DialogHeader>
   729→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   730→          </DialogHeader>
   731→          <ProjectForm
   732→            title="Create Project"
   733→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   734→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   735→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   736→                const res = await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   737→                if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   738→              }
   739→            }}
   740→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   741→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   742→          />
   743→        </DialogContent>
   744→      </Dialog>
   745→    </div>
   746→  );
   747→}
   748→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

Now let me propose approaches. Here are the key design decisions:

## Design Approach

Given your inputs, here's my recommended approach for the new default view:

**Layout per project:**
1. **Header row** — Project logo + name (clickable to detail) + description + "+ Add Source" button (links to project detail sources tab)
2. **Sources line** — "SOURCES:" label followed by inline chips (green dot + repo name + last polled age), wrapping naturally
3. **Semantic releases line** (if any) — "SEMANTIC:" label in orange, with version badges showing status + urgency chips
4. **Recent releases flow** — "RECENT:" label, releases grouped by date as flowing inline text. Up to 8 releases, then "more..." link
5. Bottom border separator between projects

**Key design decisions:**
- **Reuse existing design system** — Fraunces for titles, DM Sans for UI text, JetBrains Mono for versions/repos. Orange accent `#e8601a`. Existing border/bg colors.
- **Date grouping** — Releases grouped by relative date (e.g. "26 days ago:") as bold inline labels, with release links flowing after. This matches the mockup's natural reading pattern.
- **Sources as chips** — Small pill-like elements with green status dot, repo name in mono, and polling age. Much more compact than the current column layout.
- **No separate release or sources columns** — Everything flows inline as text, eliminating the rigid 3-column grid.

**What changes:**
- Replace the `ProjectCard` component with a new `ProjectFlowCard` component
- Fetch 8 releases instead of 5
- The `InlineSourceForm` stays (used by compact view), but the new view just has a button linking to project detail
- No changes to API, types, or other components

Does this direction look right? Any aspects you'd like adjusted before I present the full design?

## Final Design

**Each project item has this structure:**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  [Logo] op-node  Optimism rollup node              [+ Add Source]
│                                                                 
│  SOURCES:  [● i6b2w2n6/op-geth] [● i6b2w2n6/op-node]          
│                                                                 
│  SEMANTIC:  v1.6.0 [Completed] [Low]                           
│                                                                 
│  RECENT:  104d ago: 1.16.1-celestia-e9ec322 (op-node)          
│  106d ago: 1.16.1-celestia-f5b9369 (op-node)                   
│  v1.101603.4 (op-geth)  107d ago: 1.16.1-celestia-87792e2      
│  (op-node)  more...                                             
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
```

**Details:**
- **Sources chips**: green dot + repo name (monospace). No polled age.
- **Release versions**: blue links to `/releases/{id}`, with source repo name in gray parentheses
- **Date grouping**: releases grouped by relative date as bold inline labels, flowing naturally
- **"more..." link**: goes to `/releases?project={id}`
- **"+ Add Source" button**: navigates to project detail page (sources tab)
- **Semantic releases**: only shown if they exist, version in blue badge + status + urgency chip
- **8 releases max** before "more..."
- **Uses existing design system**: Fraunces headings, DM Sans body, JetBrains Mono versions, `#e8601a` accent

**Implementation**: Replace `ProjectCard` component only. No API changes. The view toggle renames "cards" to this new default.

    27→/* ---------- Tabs ---------- */

    31→  { key: "context", label: "Context Sources" },
    32→  { key: "agent", label: "Semantic Release Settings" },
    33→] as const;
    34→
    35→type TabKey = (typeof tabs)[number]["key"];
    36→
    37→/* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    38→
    39→function formatDuration(startedAt?: string, completedAt?: string): string {
    40→  if (!startedAt) return "--";
    41→  const start = new Date(startedAt).getTime();
    42→  const end = completedAt ? new Date(completedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
    43→  const secs = Math.round((end - start) / 1000);
    44→  if (secs < 60) return `${secs}s`;
    45→  const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    46→  const rem = secs % 60;
    47→  return `${mins}m ${rem}s`;
    48→}
    49→
    50→function truncate(str: string, max: number): string {
    51→  return str.length > max ? str.slice(0, max) + "\u2026" : str;
    52→}
    53→
    54→/* ---------- Main component ---------- */
    55→
    56→export function ProjectDetail() {
    57→  // Read ID from URL path — useParams() returns stale "0" in static export
    58→  const id = getPathSegment(1); // /projects/{id}
    59→  const router = useRouter();
    60→  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabKey>("sources");
    61→
    62→  /* Test run state */

    64→  const [testVersion, setTestVersion] = useState<string>("");
    65→  const [testRunning, setTestRunning] = useState(false);
    66→
    67→  /* Agent config local state */
    68→  const [promptDraft, setPromptDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
    69→  const [rulesDraft, setRulesDraft] = useState<AgentRules | null>(null);
    70→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    71→
    72→  /* Inline edit state */

    74→  const [editingDesc, setEditingDesc] = useState(false);
    75→  const [nameDraft, setNameDraft] = useState("");
    76→  const [descDraft, setDescDraft] = useState("");
    77→  const nameRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    78→  const descRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    79→

   360→      </div>
   361→
   362→      {/* ===== Tab content ===== */}
   363→      <div className="pt-6">
   364→        {/* --- Sources tab --- */}
   365→        {activeTab === "sources" && (
   366→          <div>
   367→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   368→              <SectionLabel>Sources</SectionLabel>
   369→              <button
   370→                onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}
   371→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   372→                style={{
   373→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   374→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   375→                  color: "#374151",
   376→                }}
   377→              >
   378→                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   379→                Add Source
   380→              </button>
   381→            </div>
   382→
   383→            {sourcesData?.data && sourcesData.data.length > 0 ? (
   384→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   385→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   386→                  <thead>
   387→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   388→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Provider</th>
   389→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Repository</th>
   390→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Interval</th>
   391→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   392→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Last polled</th>
   393→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   394→                    </tr>
   395→                  </thead>
   396→                  <tbody>
   397→                    {sourcesData.data.map((source) => (
   398→                      <tr key={source.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   399→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   400→                          <ProviderBadge provider={source.provider} />
   401→                        </td>
   402→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   403→                          <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: "12px" }}>
   404→                            {source.repository}
   405→                          </span>
   406→                        </td>
   407→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>
   408→                          {formatInterval(source.poll_interval_seconds)}
   409→                        </td>
   410→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   411→                          <button
   412→                            onClick={() => handleToggleSource(source)}
   413→                            className="relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full transition-colors duration-200"
   414→                            style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   415→                            title={source.enabled ? "Disable polling" : "Enable polling"}
   416→                          >
   417→                            <span
   418→                              className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform duration-200"
   419→                              style={{ transform: source.enabled ? "translateX(18px)" : "translateX(3px)" }}
   420→                            />
   421→                          </button>
   422→                        </td>
   423→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   424→                          {source.last_polled_at
   425→                            ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString()
   426→                            : "Never"}
   427→                        </td>
   428→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   429→                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
   430→                            <button
   431→                              onClick={() => setEditingSource(source)}
   432→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1] hover:text-[#111113]"
   433→                              title="Edit source"
   434→                            >
   435→                              <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
   436→                            </button>
   437→                            <button
   438→                              onClick={() => setDeletingSourceId(source.id)}
   439→                              className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   440→                              title="Delete source"
   441→                            >
   442→                              <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   443→                            </button>
   444→                          </div>
   445→                        </td>
   446→                      </tr>
   447→                    ))}
   448→                  </tbody>
   449→                </table>
   450→              </div>
   451→            ) : (
   452→              <div
   453→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   454→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   455→              >
   456→                No sources configured
   457→              </div>
   458→            )}
   459→          </div>
   460→        )}
   461→
   462→        {/* --- Context Sources tab --- */}
   463→        {activeTab === "context" && (
   464→          <div>
   465→            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   466→              <SectionLabel>Context Sources</SectionLabel>
   467→              <button
   468→                onClick={() => setCtxCreateOpen(true)}
   469→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   470→                style={{
   471→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   472→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   473→                  color: "#374151",
   474→                }}
   475→              >
   476→                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   477→                Add Context Source
   478→              </button>
   479→            </div>
   480→
   481→            {ctxData?.data && ctxData.data.length > 0 ? (
   482→              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   483→                <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   484→                  <thead>
   485→                    <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   486→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Type</th>
   487→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Name</th>
   488→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Config URL</th>
   489→                      <th className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}></th>
   490→                    </tr>
   491→                  </thead>
   492→                  <tbody>
   493→                    {ctxData.data.map((ctx) => (
   494→                      <tr key={ctx.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   495→                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
   496→                          <span
   497→                            className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none"
   498→                            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", color: "#374151" }}
   499→                          >
   500→                            {ctx.type}
   501→                          </span>
   502→                        </td>
   503→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium" style={{ color: "#111113" }}>
   504→                          {ctx.name}
   505→                        </td>
   506→                        <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   507→                          <span
   508→                            className="text-[12px]"
   509→                            style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
   510→                            title={typeof ctx.config?.url === "string" ? ctx.config.url : ""}
   511→                          >
   512→                            {typeof ctx.config?.url === "string"
   513→                              ? truncate(ctx.config.url, 50)
   514→                              : "--"}
   515→                          </span>
   516→                        </td>
   517→                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
   518→                          <button
   519→                            onClick={() => setDeletingCtxId(ctx.id)}
   520→                            className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   521→                            title="Delete context source"
   522→                          >
   523→                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   524→                          </button>
   525→                        </td>
   526→                      </tr>
   527→                    ))}
   528→                  </tbody>
   529→                </table>
   530→              </div>
   531→            ) : (
   532→              <div
   533→                className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   534→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   535→              >
   536→                No context sources configured
   537→              </div>
   538→            )}
   539→          </div>
   540→        )}
   541→
   542→        {/* --- Semantic Release Settings tab --- */}
   543→        {activeTab === "agent" && (
   544→          <div className="space-y-6">
   545→            {/* Card 1: Trigger Rules */}
   546→            <div
   547→              className="rounded-lg border p-5"
   548→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   549→            >
   550→              <SectionLabel className="mb-1">Trigger Rules</SectionLabel>
   551→              <p className="mb-4 text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   552→                Automatically run the agent when new releases match these conditions.
   553→              </p>
   554→              <div className="space-y-3">
   555→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   556→                  <input
   557→                    type="checkbox"
   558→                    checked={currentRules.on_major_release ?? false}
   559→                    onChange={(e) =>
   560→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_major_release: e.target.checked })
   561→                    }
   562→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   563→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   564→                  />
   565→                  Major release
   566→                  <span className="text-[11px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   567→                    (e.g. 1.x &rarr; 2.x)
   568→                  </span>
   569→                </label>
   570→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   571→                  <input
   572→                    type="checkbox"
   573→                    checked={currentRules.on_minor_release ?? false}
   574→                    onChange={(e) =>
   575→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_minor_release: e.target.checked })
   576→                    }
   577→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   578→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   579→                  />
   580→                  Minor release
   581→                  <span className="text-[11px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   582→                    (e.g. 1.1 &rarr; 1.2)
   583→                  </span>
   584→                </label>
   585→                <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   586→                  <input
   587→                    type="checkbox"
   588→                    checked={currentRules.on_security_patch ?? false}
   589→                    onChange={(e) =>
   590→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, on_security_patch: e.target.checked })
   591→                    }
   592→                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border accent-[#e8601a]"
   593→                    style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}
   594→                  />
   595→                  Security patch
   596→                  <span className="text-[11px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   597→                    (contains security/CVE keywords)
   598→                  </span>
   599→                </label>
   600→                <div className="pt-2">
   601→                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   602→                    Version pattern
   603→                  </label>
   604→                  <input
   605→                    type="text"
   606→                    value={currentRules.version_pattern ?? ""}
   607→                    onChange={(e) =>
   608→                      setRulesDraft({ ...currentRules, version_pattern: e.target.value })
   609→                    }
   610→                    placeholder="e.g. ^v\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$"
   611→                    className="w-full max-w-md rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   612→                    style={{
   613→                      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   614→                      backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   615→                      borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   616→                      color: "#111113",
   617→                    }}
   618→                  />
   619→                  <p className="mt-1 text-[11px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   620→                    Optional regex to filter which versions trigger agent runs.
   621→                  </p>
   622→                </div>
   623→              </div>
   624→            </div>
   625→
   626→            {/* Card 2: Agent Prompt */}
   627→            <div
   628→              className="rounded-lg border p-5"
   629→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   630→            >
   631→              <SectionLabel className="mb-1">Agent Prompt</SectionLabel>
   632→              <p className="mb-3 text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   633→                Custom instructions for the agent when analyzing releases.
   634→              </p>
   635→              <textarea
   636→                value={currentPrompt}
   637→                onChange={(e) => setPromptDraft(e.target.value)}
   638→                rows={5}
   639→                placeholder="Using default agent prompt."
   640→                className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] placeholder:text-[#9ca3af] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   641→                style={{
   642→                  fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   643→                  backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   644→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   645→                  color: "#111113",
   646→                }}
   647→              />
   648→              <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end">
   649→                <button
   650→                  onClick={handleSaveAgentConfig}
   651→                  disabled={saving || (promptDraft === null && rulesDraft === null)}
   652→                  className="rounded-md px-4 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   653→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   654→                >
   655→                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save Settings"}
   656→                </button>
   657→              </div>
   658→            </div>
   659→
   660→            {/* Card 3: Test Run */}
   661→            <div
   662→              className="rounded-lg border p-5"
   663→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   664→            >
   665→              <SectionLabel className="mb-1">Test Run</SectionLabel>
   666→              <p className="mb-4 text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   667→                Trigger a one-off agent run to test your configuration against a specific release.
   668→              </p>
   669→              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
   670→                <div className="min-w-[200px] flex-1">
   671→                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   672→                    Source
   673→                  </label>
   674→                  <select
   675→                    value={testSourceId}
   676→                    onChange={(e) => {
   677→                      setTestSourceId(e.target.value);
   678→                      setTestVersion("");
   679→                    }}
   680→                    className="w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1"
   681→                    style={{
   682→                      fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif",
   683→                      backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   684→                      borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   685→                      color: testSourceId ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   686→                    }}
   687→                  >
   688→                    <option value="">Select a source...</option>
   689→                    {sourcesData?.data?.map((s) => (
   690→                      <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
   691→                        {s.provider}: {s.repository}
   692→                      </option>
   693→                    ))}
   694→                  </select>
   695→                </div>
   696→                <div className="min-w-[200px] flex-1">
   697→                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px]" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   698→                    Version
   699→                  </label>
   700→                  <select
   701→                    value={testVersion}
   702→                    onChange={(e) => setTestVersion(e.target.value)}
   703→                    disabled={!testSourceId}
   704→                    className="w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 disabled:opacity-50"
   705→                    style={{
   706→                      fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   707→                      backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   708→                      borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   709→                      color: testVersion ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   710→                    }}
   711→                  >
   712→                    <option value="">
   713→                      {testSourceId ? "Select a version..." : "Choose a source first"}
   714→                    </option>
   715→                    {testReleasesData?.data?.map((r) => (
   716→                      <option key={r.id} value={r.version}>
   717→                        {r.version}
   718→                      </option>
   719→                    ))}
   720→                  </select>
   721→                </div>
   722→                <button
   723→                  onClick={handleTestRun}
   724→                  disabled={testRunning || !testVersion}
   725→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   726→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a" }}
   727→                >
   728→                  <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   729→                  {testRunning ? "Running..." : "Run Test"}
   730→                </button>
   731→              </div>
   732→            </div>
   733→
   734→            {/* Card 4: Run History */}
   735→            <div>
   736→              <SectionLabel className="mb-3">Run History</SectionLabel>
   737→              {runsData?.data && runsData.data.length > 0 ? (
   738→                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   739→                  <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans), sans-serif" }}>
   740→                    <thead>
   741→                      <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   742→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Trigger</th>
   743→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Status</th>
   744→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Started</th>
   745→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Duration</th>
   746→                        <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>Semantic Release</th>
   747→                      </tr>
   748→                    </thead>
   749→                    <tbody>
   750→                      {runsData.data.map((run) => (
   751→                        <tr key={run.id} className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   752→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#374151" }}>
   753→                            {run.trigger}
   754→                          </td>
   755→                          <td className="px-4 py-3">
   756→                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   757→                              <StatusDot status={run.status} />
   758→                              <span style={{ color: "#6b7280" }}>{run.status}</span>
   759→                            </div>
   760→                          </td>
   761→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   762→                            {run.started_at
   763→                              ? new Date(run.started_at).toLocaleString()
   764→                              : "Pending"}
   765→                          </td>
   766→                          <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   767→                            {formatDuration(run.started_at, run.completed_at)}
   768→                          </td>
   769→                          <td className="px-4 py-3">
   770→                            {run.semantic_release_id ? (
   771→                              <Link
   772→                                href={`/projects/${id}/semantic-releases/${run.semantic_release_id}`}
   773→                                className="text-[13px] font-medium underline"
   774→                                style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   775→                              >
   776→                                View
   777→                              </Link>
   778→                            ) : (
   779→                              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>--</span>
   780→                            )}
   781→                          </td>
   782→                        </tr>
   783→                      ))}
   784→                    </tbody>
   785→                  </table>
   786→                </div>
   787→              ) : (
   788→                <div
   789→                  className="flex h-32 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-[13px]"
   790→                  style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#9ca3af" }}
   791→                >
   792→                  No agent runs yet
   793→                </div>
   794→              )}
   795→            </div>
   796→          </div>
   797→        )}
   798→      </div>
   799→
   800→      {/* Source create dialog */}
   801→      <Dialog open={sourceCreateOpen} onOpenChange={setSourceCreateOpen}>
   802→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   803→          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Add Source</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
   804→          <SourceForm
   805→            title="Add Source"
   806→            projectId={id}
   807→            onSubmit={async (input) => { const res = await sourcesApi.create(id, input); if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {}); }}
   808→            onSuccess={() => { setSourceCreateOpen(false); mutateSources(); }}
   809→            onCancel={() => setSourceCreateOpen(false)}
   810→          />
   811→        </DialogContent>
   812→      </Dialog>
   813→
   814→      {/* Source edit dialog */}
   815→      <Dialog open={!!editingSource} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingSource(null); }}>
   816→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   817→          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Edit Source</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
   818→          {editingSource && (
   819→            <SourceForm
   820→              key={editingSource.id}
   821→              title="Edit Source"
   822→              initial={editingSource}
   823→              onSubmit={async (input) => { await sourcesApi.update(editingSource.id, input); }}
   824→              onSuccess={() => { setEditingSource(null); mutateSources(); }}
   825→              onCancel={() => setEditingSource(null)}
   826→            />
   827→          )}
   828→        </DialogContent>
   829→      </Dialog>
   830→
   831→      {/* Source delete dialog */}
   832→      <ConfirmDialog
   833→        open={!!deletingSourceId}
   834→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDeletingSourceId(null); }}
   835→        title="Delete Source"
   836→        description="This will permanently delete this source and its releases."
   837→        onConfirm={async () => { if (deletingSourceId) { await sourcesApi.delete(deletingSourceId); mutateSources(); } }}
   838→      />

    25→
    26→export function SourceForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, redirectTo, onSuccess, onCancel }: SourceFormProps) {
    27→  const router = useRouter();
    28→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    29→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    30→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(initial?.provider ?? "dockerhub");
    31→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState(initial?.repository ?? "");
    32→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState(String(initial?.poll_interval_seconds ?? 86400));
    33→  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);
    34→  const [configJson, setConfigJson] = useState(
    35→    JSON.stringify(initial?.config ?? {}, null, 2)
    36→  );
    37→  const [versionFilterInclude, setVersionFilterInclude] = useState(initial?.version_filter_include ?? "");
    38→  const [versionFilterExclude, setVersionFilterExclude] = useState(initial?.version_filter_exclude ?? "");
    39→  const [excludePrereleases, setExcludePrereleases] = useState(initial?.exclude_prereleases ?? false);
    40→
    41→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    42→    e.preventDefault();
    43→    setError("");
    44→
    45→    const repoError = validateRepository(provider, repository);
    46→    if (repoError) {
    47→      setError(repoError);
    48→      return;
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    let parsedConfig: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    52→    if (configJson.trim() && configJson.trim() !== "{}") {
    53→      try {
    54→        parsedConfig = JSON.parse(configJson);
    55→      } catch {
    56→        setError("Config must be valid JSON");
    57→        return;
    58→      }
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    setSaving(true);
    62→    try {
    63→      await onSubmit({
    64→        provider,
    65→        repository: repository.trim(),
    66→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval),
    67→        enabled,
    68→        config: parsedConfig,
    69→        version_filter_include: versionFilterInclude.trim() || undefined,
    70→        version_filter_exclude: versionFilterExclude.trim() || undefined,
    71→        exclude_prereleases: excludePrereleases || undefined,
    72→      });
    73→      if (onSuccess) {
    74→        onSuccess();
    75→      } else {
    76→        router.push(redirectTo ?? "/sources");
    77→      }
    78→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    79→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    80→    } finally {
    81→      setSaving(false);
    82→    }
    83→  };
    84→
    85→  const handleCancel = () => {
    86→    if (onCancel) {
    87→      onCancel();
    88→    } else {
    89→      router.back();
    90→    }
    91→  };
    92→
    93→  const formContent = (
    94→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    95→      {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
    96→      <div className="space-y-2">
    97→        <Label>Provider</Label>
    98→        <Select value={provider} onValueChange={setProvider}>
    99→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
   100→          <SelectContent>
   101→            <SelectItem value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</SelectItem>
   102→            <SelectItem value="github">GitHub</SelectItem>
   103→            <SelectItem value="ecr-public">ECR Public</SelectItem>
   104→            <SelectItem value="gitlab">GitLab</SelectItem>
   105→          </SelectContent>
   106→        </Select>
   107→      </div>
   108→      <div className="space-y-2">
   109→        <Label htmlFor="repository">Repository</Label>
   110→        <Input id="repository" value={repository} onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. library/golang or ethereum/go-ethereum" required />
   111→      </div>
   112→      <div className="space-y-2">
   113→        <Label htmlFor="poll_interval">Poll Interval (seconds)</Label>
   114→        <Input id="poll_interval" type="number" min={60} value={pollInterval} onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)} />
   115→      </div>
   116→      <div className="space-y-2">
   117→        <Label htmlFor="config">Config (JSON, optional)</Label>
   118→        <Textarea
   119→          id="config"
   120→          value={configJson}
   121→          onChange={(e) => setConfigJson(e.target.value)}
   122→          rows={4}
   123→          className="font-mono text-sm"
   124→          placeholder="{}"
   125→        />
   126→      </div>
   127→      <div className="space-y-2">
   128→        <Label htmlFor="version_filter_include">Version Filter — Include (regex, optional)</Label>
   129→        <Input
   130→          id="version_filter_include"
   131→          value={versionFilterInclude}
   132→          onChange={(e) => setVersionFilterInclude(e.target.value)}
   133→          placeholder='e.g. ^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$'
   134→          className="font-mono text-sm"
   135→        />
   136→        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Only show/notify versions matching this pattern</p>
   137→      </div>
   138→      <div className="space-y-2">
   139→        <Label htmlFor="version_filter_exclude">Version Filter — Exclude (regex, optional)</Label>
   140→        <Input
   141→          id="version_filter_exclude"
   142→          value={versionFilterExclude}
   143→          onChange={(e) => setVersionFilterExclude(e.target.value)}
   144→          placeholder='e.g. -(alpha|beta|rc|nightly)'
   145→          className="font-mono text-sm"
   146→        />
   147→        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Hide/suppress versions matching this pattern</p>
   148→      </div>
   149→      {(provider === "github" || provider === "gitlab") && (
   150→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   151→          <Switch checked={excludePrereleases} onCheckedChange={setExcludePrereleases} />
   152→          <Label>Exclude Pre-Releases</Label>
   153→        </div>
   154→      )}
   155→      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   156→        <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />
   157→        <Label>Enabled</Label>
   158→      </div>
   159→      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   160→        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={handleCancel}>Cancel</Button>
   161→        <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   162→      </div>
   163→    </form>
   164→  );
   165→
   166→  if (onSuccess) {
   167→    return formContent;
   168→  }
   169→
   170→  return (
   171→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
   172→      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>
   173→      <CardContent>
   174→        {formContent}
   175→      </CardContent>
   176→    </Card>
   177→  );
   178→}

I'll reuse this exact same `SourceForm` + `Dialog` pattern in the new flow card view. The "+ Add Source" button will open a dialog with `SourceForm` right there on the projects page.

The design is now fully clarified. Let me present the final summary:

## Final Design Summary

**Per project:**
1. **Header**: Logo + name (link to detail) + description + "+ Add Source" button (opens `SourceForm` dialog)
2. **Sources**: "SOURCES:" label + inline chips (green dot + repo name in mono + polled age)
3. **Semantic** (if any): "SEMANTIC:" label (orange) + version badge + status + urgency chip
4. **Recent**: "RECENT:" label + up to 8 releases flowing inline, each: version link (to `/releases/{id}`) + (source name) + relative date. Then "more..." link to `/releases?project={id}`

**Implementation**: Replace `ProjectCard` with `ProjectFlowCard`. Reuse `SourceForm` in a dialog. No API changes.

Ready to proceed with implementation?

- **Click a source chip** → opens `SourceForm` dialog pre-filled with that source's data (edit mode)
- **"+ Add Source" button** → opens `SourceForm` dialog empty (create mode)

     1→export function timeAgo(dateStr?: string | null): string {
     2→  if (!dateStr) return "\u2014";
     3→  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime();
     4→  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
     5→  if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`;
     6→  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
     7→  if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`;
     8→  return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export function validateRepository(provider: string, repo: string): string | null {
    12→  const trimmed = repo.trim();
    13→  if (!trimmed) return null;
    14→  if (provider === "github" && /^(https?:\/\/)?github\.com\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    15→    return "Use owner/repo format (e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum), not a full URL";
    16→  }
    17→  if (provider === "dockerhub" && /^(https?:\/\/)?hub\.docker\.com\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    18→    return "Use owner/image format (e.g. library/nginx), not a full URL";
    19→  }
    20→  if (provider === "ecr-public" && /^(https?:\/\/)?(gallery\.ecr\.aws|public\.ecr\.aws)\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    21→    return "Use alias/repo format (e.g. i6b2w2n6/op-node), not a full URL";
    22→  }
    23→  if (provider === "gitlab" && /^(https?:\/\/)?gitlab\.com\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    24→    return "Use owner/repo format (e.g. inkscape/inkscape), not a full URL";
    25→  }
    26→  if (/^https?:\/\//.test(trimmed)) {
    27→    return "Use owner/repo format, not a full URL";
    28→  }
    29→  return null;
    30→}
    31→
    32→export function formatInterval(seconds: number): string {
    33→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    34→  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
    35→  if (seconds < 86400) return `${(seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`;
    36→  return `${(seconds / 86400).toFixed(1)}d`;
    37→}
    38→

## Summary

### What was implemented

Replaced the old multi-panel `ProjectCard` component with the new `ProjectFlowCard` component in the projects listing page. The new component renders a more compact, flow-style card that shows sources as clickable chips, semantic releases inline with urgency badges, and recent releases in a flowing text layout -- all within a single flat card instead of a three-column grid.

### File changed

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx`**

### Changes made

1. **Added import**: `SourceForm` from `@/components/sources/source-form` (line 17)
2. **Removed unused imports**: `X` from lucide-react, `validateRepository` and `formatInterval` from `@/lib/format` (line 15-16)
3. **Removed old components** (5 functions deleted):
   - `InlineSourceForm` -- replaced by `SourceForm` in a dialog
   - `SourcesSection` -- inlined as source chips in the flow card
   - `RecentReleasesSection` -- inlined as flowing release links
   - `SemanticReleasesSection` -- inlined as semantic release badges
