I now have all the information needed to produce a thorough review. Here is my assessment.

---

# Code Review: ProjectFlowCard Implementation

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx`
**Plan:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/plans/2026-03-02-projects-flow-view.md`

---

## Plan Alignment

The implementation matches the plan exactly. Every step described in the plan document has been faithfully executed:

- SourceForm import added (line 17)
- Old components (`InlineSourceForm`, `SourcesSection`, `RecentReleasesSection`, `SemanticReleasesSection`, `ProjectCard`) removed
- New `ProjectFlowCard` component written with sources chips, semantic releases inline, recent releases as flowing text, and dialog integrations
- `ProjectCompactRow` preserved unchanged
- Render reference updated from `ProjectCard` to `ProjectFlowCard` (line 527)
- Spacing changed from `space-y-4` to `space-y-5` (line 524)
- Unused imports cleaned up

No deviations from the plan.

---

## Strengths

1. **Smart SWR cache sharing.** `ProjectCardLogo` (line 32) and `ProjectFlowCard` (line 45) use the identical SWR key `project-${projectId}-card-sources`. SWR deduplicates these into a single network request. This is intentional and well-done.

2. **Correct key usage on all lists.** Every `.map()` call uses stable, unique `id` fields as keys (lines 115, 151, 200, 525-529). No index-based keys.

3. **Proper SourceForm integration.** The edit dialog uses `key={editingSource.id}` (line 269) to force a fresh form instance when switching between sources. The conditional render `{editingSource && (...)}` (line 266) avoids passing stale initial data. Both create and edit dialogs correctly call `mutateSources()` on success to refresh the sources list.

4. **Clean component decomposition.** The file has a clear structure: constants, then helper component, then flow card, then compact row, then page. Section comments make navigation straightforward.

5. **Type safety.** All types are properly imported and used. TypeScript compiles cleanly with zero errors.

6. **Defensive data access.** Nullish coalescing on all SWR results (`srcData?.data ?? []`, lines 57-59) prevents crashes when data is loading.

---

## Issues

### Important (should fix)

**1. `sourceMap` is reconstructed on every render without memoization**

Lines 61-62 and 307-308:
```typescript
const sourceMap = new Map<string, Source>();
for (const s of sources) sourceMap.set(s.id, s);
```

This Map is rebuilt on every render, even when `sources` has not changed. With a small number of sources per project (typically < 20) this is not a performance problem today, but it is an unnecessary allocation during unrelated re-renders (e.g., when the dialog state changes). Wrapping in `useMemo` would be the standard React practice:

```typescript
const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
  const m = new Map<string, Source>();
  for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
  return m;
}, [sources]);
```

This requires adding `useMemo` to the React import on line 3. The same applies to the identical pattern in `ProjectCompactRow` (lines 307-308).

**Severity:** Important -- not a bug, but it is an anti-pattern that any React reviewer would flag. With 12 cards visible per page, each card rebuilds this map on every render cycle.

---

**2. Non-null assertion on `sr.report!.urgency` (line 179)**

```typescript
{sr.report!.urgency}
```

The `urgencyStyle` guard on line 152-154 checks `sr.report?.urgency`, so if we reach line 179 it means `sr.report` and `sr.report.urgency` are both truthy. The logic is safe at runtime, but the non-null assertion `!` suppresses the compiler instead of proving safety. A minor change to use optional chaining would be cleaner:

```typescript
{sr.report?.urgency}
```

This reads identically at runtime but avoids the `!` operator, which is generally treated as a code smell in TypeScript codebases.

---

**3. Error handling gap in SourceForm `onSubmit` callbacks**

Lines 244-247 (create dialog):
```typescript
onSubmit={async (input) => {
  const res = await sourcesApi.create(project.id, input);
  if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
}}
```

If `sourcesApi.create()` throws, the error propagates into `SourceForm`'s `handleSubmit` which catches and displays it. That is fine. However, `sourcesApi.poll` errors are silently swallowed with `.catch(() => {})`. This is consistent with how it is done in the `ProjectForm` handler on line 573, so it is a codebase-wide pattern. Still worth noting: if polling fails, there is no indication to the user at all.

---

### Minor (nice to have)

**4. Hardcoded inline styles vs. Tailwind or CSS variables**

The component uses a mix of Tailwind classes and `style={{...}}` props with hardcoded hex colors (e.g., `#e8e8e5`, `#e8601a`, `#f3f3f1`, `#374151`). This is consistent with the rest of the codebase -- the same pattern appears in `ProjectCompactRow` and the page component. It is not a regression, but centralizing the brand color palette (especially `#e8601a` which appears 6 times in this file) into CSS variables or a constants file would improve maintainability long-term. This is a systemic concern, not specific to this PR.

**5. Accessibility: source chips lack descriptive labels**

Line 116-137: The source chip buttons use `onClick` and show repository names, but lack `aria-label` or `title` attributes that would indicate "Click to edit source". A screen reader user would hear the repository name but not know the button opens an edit dialog.

```typescript
<button
  key={source.id}
  onClick={() => setEditingSource(source)}
  aria-label={`Edit source ${source.repository}`}
  // ...
>
```

Similarly, the "Add Source" button (line 92-103) could benefit from `aria-label="Add source to project"` for disambiguation when multiple cards are visible.

**6. The `"more..."` link uses a typographic ellipsis character**

Line 230:
```typescript
more…
```

This is fine and intentional -- using the Unicode ellipsis character (`\u2026`) is the typographically correct choice. No issue here, just noting it was examined.

---

## Architecture and Design

- **Separation of concerns:** The component handles its own data fetching (SWR) and dialog state. This is consistent with the file's existing pattern where each card component owns its data. An alternative would be to lift data fetching to the parent and pass via props, but that would break the current architecture pattern and is not warranted.

- **SOLID adherence:** The `SourceForm` reuse for both create and edit modes demonstrates good adherence to DRY/Open-Closed. The form itself handles the `initial` prop to switch between modes without modification.

- **Dialog per card instance:** Each `ProjectFlowCard` renders two Dialog components (create and edit). With 12 cards per page, that is 24 Dialog DOM nodes. Since the dialogs use conditional rendering (`open` prop controls visibility, not mounting), the actual DOM impact is minimal -- shadcn Dialog uses Radix which portal-mounts only when open. This is acceptable.

---

## Assessment: **Approved with minor fixes**

The implementation is clean, follows the plan exactly, integrates correctly with existing patterns, and compiles without errors. The two items I would recommend fixing before merging:

1. Wrap `sourceMap` construction in `useMemo` (Important -- both in `ProjectFlowCard` line 61 and `ProjectCompactRow` line 307)
2. Replace `sr.report!.urgency` with `sr.report?.urgency` on line 179 (Important -- avoids non-null assertions)

Everything else is either consistent with existing codebase patterns or is a low-priority enhancement.

   290→/* ---------- Compact Row ---------- */
   291→
   292→function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   293→  const router = useRouter();
   294→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-releases`, () =>
   295→    releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 5)
   296→  );
   297→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sources`, () =>
   298→    sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id)
   299→  );
   300→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sr`, () =>
   301→    srApi.list(project.id, 1, 3)
   302→  );
   303→
   304→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   305→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   306→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   307→  const latest = releases[0];
   308→  const latestSr = srItems[0];
   309→
   310→  const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
   311→    const m = new Map<string, Source>();
   312→    for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
   313→    return m;
   314→  }, [sources]);
   315→
   316→  const latestSrc = latest ? sourceMap.get(latest.source_id) : undefined;
   317→  const latestIcon = latestSrc ? getProviderIcon(latestSrc.provider) : undefined;
   318→
   319→  const urgencyStyle = latestSr?.report?.urgency
   320→    ? URGENCY_COLORS[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   321→    : undefined;
   322→
   323→  return (
   324→    <div
   325→      onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}
   326→      className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#f9f9f7] cursor-pointer"
   327→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   328→    >
   329→      {/* Project name */}
   330→      <span
   331→        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-bold shrink-0"
   332→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a", minWidth: "160px" }}
   333→      >
   334→        <ProjectLogo name={project.name} sources={sources} size={24} />
   335→        {project.name}
   336→      </span>
   337→
   338→      {/* Latest release */}
   339→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ minWidth: "140px" }}>
   340→        {latest ? (
   341→          <>
   342→            <Link
   343→              href={`/releases/${latest.id}`}
   344→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   345→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-300 transition-shadow"
   346→              style={{
   347→                backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   348→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   349→                color: "#374151",
   350→              }}
   351→            >
   352→              {latest.version}
   353→            </Link>
   354→            {latestIcon && latestIcon({ size: 12, className: "shrink-0", style: { color: "#9ca3af" } })}
   355→          </>
   356→        ) : (
   357→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   358→            No releases
   359→          </span>
   360→        )}
   361→      </span>
   362→
   363→      {/* Latest semantic release */}
   364→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   365→        {latestSr ? (
   366→          <>
   367→            <Link
   368→              href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   369→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   370→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none shrink-0 hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   371→              style={{
   372→                backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   373→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   374→                color: "#1d4ed8",
   375→              }}
   376→            >
   377→              {latestSr.version}
   378→            </Link>
   379→            {urgencyStyle && (
   380→              <span
   381→                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none shrink-0"
   382→                style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   383→              >
   384→                {latestSr.report?.urgency}
   385→              </span>
   386→            )}
   387→            {latestSr.report?.summary && (
   388→              <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   389→                {latestSr.report.summary.length > 60
   390→                  ? latestSr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   391→                  : latestSr.report.summary}
   392→              </span>
   393→            )}
   394→          </>
   395→        ) : (
   396→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   397→            No analysis
   398→          </span>
   399→        )}
   400→      </span>
   401→
   402→      {/* Arrow */}
   403→      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }} />
   404→    </div>
   405→  );
   406→}

   410→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   411→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   412→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
   413→  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

   418→  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

   420→    ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
   421→    : items;
   422→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   423→  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
   424→  const paged = filtered.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);
   425→
   426→  return (
   427→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   428→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   429→        <div>
   430→          <h1
   431→            style={{
   432→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   433→              fontSize: "24px",
   434→              fontWeight: 700,
   435→              color: "#111113",

   438→            Projects

   441→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   442→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}

   450→            <input
   451→              type="text"

   453→              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
   454→              placeholder="Search projects..."
   455→              className="rounded-md border py-1.5 pl-8 pr-3 text-[13px] w-48"
   456→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   457→            />
   458→          </div>
   459→          <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   460→            <button
   461→              onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
   462→              className="p-1.5 rounded-l-md transition-colors"
   463→              style={{
   464→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "cards" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   465→                color: viewMode === "cards" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   466→              }}
   467→              title="Card view"
   468→            >

   470→            </button>
   471→            <button
   472→              onClick={() => setViewMode("compact")}

   474→              style={{

   476→                color: viewMode === "compact" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",

   478→              title="Compact view"

   512→          <button
   513→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   514→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   515→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   516→          >
   517→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   518→            New Project
   519→          </button>
   520→        </div>
   521→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   522→        <p
   523→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   524→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   525→        >
   526→          No projects matching &ldquo;{search}&rdquo;
   527→        </p>
   528→      ) : (
   529→        <>
   530→          <div className={viewMode === "cards" ? "space-y-5" : "space-y-1.5"}>
   531→            {paged.map((project) =>
   532→              viewMode === "cards" ? (
   533→                <ProjectFlowCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   534→              ) : (
   535→                <ProjectCompactRow key={project.id} project={project} />
   536→              )
   537→            )}
   538→          </div>
   539→          {totalPages > 1 && (
   540→            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
   541→              <button
   542→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
   543→                disabled={currentPage <= 1}
   544→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   545→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   546→              >
   547→                Previous
   548→              </button>
   549→              <span
   550→                className="text-[13px]"
   551→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   552→              >
   553→                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
   554→              </span>
   555→              <button
   556→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
   557→                disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
   558→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   559→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   560→              >
   561→                Next
   562→              </button>
   563→            </div>
   564→          )}
   565→        </>
   566→      )}
   567→
   568→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   569→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   570→          <DialogHeader>
   571→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   572→          </DialogHeader>
   573→          <ProjectForm
   574→            title="Create Project"
   575→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   576→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   577→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   578→                const res = await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   579→                if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   580→              }
   581→            }}
   582→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   583→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   584→          />
   585→        </DialogContent>
   586→      </Dialog>
   587→    </div>
   588→  );
   589→}
   590→

This requires measuring the container width and dynamically fitting as many releases as will fit on ~2 lines, then showing "more..." for the overflow. Let me think about the right approach.

The cleanest way is to use a container ref with `ResizeObserver`, render all items but hide overflow beyond a calculated height (roughly 2 lines of the flowing text), and show "more..." when there's overflow. This avoids guessing widths per item.

Good, I have the full picture. The approach: fetch more items from the API (e.g. 25 instead of 8/3), render all items into a container with `max-height` set to ~2 lines, use a `ref` + `useEffect` to detect overflow, and show "more..." only when overflowing. This is simple, CSS-driven, and adapts to any screen width.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "/* ---------- Project Card Logo ---------- */\n\nfunction ProjectCardLogo({ projectId, name }: { projectId: string; name: string }) {\n  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>\n    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)\n  );\n  return <ProjectLogo name={name} sources={data?.data} size={40} />;\n}",
  "new_string": "/* ---------- Project Card Logo ---------- */\n\nfunction ProjectCardLogo({ projectId, name }: { projectId: string; name: string }) {\n  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>\n    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)\n  );\n  return <ProjectLogo name={name} sources={data?.data} size={40} />;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  labelColor,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  labelColor?: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const innerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [overflows, setOverflows] = useState(false);\n\n  const check = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = innerRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    setOverflows(el.scrollHeight > LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES + 4);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    check();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(check);\n    if (innerRef.current) obs.observe(innerRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [check, children]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"text-[13px] leading-7\">\n      <div\n        ref={innerRef}\n        style={{ maxHeight: LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES, overflow: \"hidden\" }}\n      >\n        <span\n          className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\"\n          style={{ color: labelColor ?? \"#9ca3af\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {children}\n      </div>\n      {overflows && (\n        <Link\n          href={moreHref}\n          className=\"text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline\"\n          style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n        >\n          more…\n        </Link>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

  "old_string": "/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  labelColor,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  labelColor?: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const innerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [overflows, setOverflows] = useState(false);\n\n  const check = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = innerRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    setOverflows(el.scrollHeight > LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES + 4);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    check();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(check);\n    if (innerRef.current) obs.observe(innerRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [check, children]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"text-[13px] leading-7\">\n      <div\n        ref={innerRef}\n        style={{ maxHeight: LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES, overflow: \"hidden\" }}\n      >\n        <span\n          className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\"\n          style={{ color: labelColor ?? \"#9ca3af\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {children}\n      </div>\n      {overflows && (\n        <Link\n          href={moreHref}\n          className=\"text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline\"\n          style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n        >\n          more…\n        </Link>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const innerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [overflows, setOverflows] = useState(false);\n\n  const check = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = innerRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    setOverflows(el.scrollHeight > LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES + 4);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    check();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(check);\n    if (innerRef.current) obs.observe(innerRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [check, children]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative text-[13px] leading-7\">\n      <div\n        ref={innerRef}\n        style={{ maxHeight: LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES, overflow: \"hidden\" }}\n      >\n        <span\n          className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\"\n          style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {children}\n      </div>\n      {overflows && (\n        <Link\n          href={moreHref}\n          className=\"absolute right-0 text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline pl-6\"\n          style={{\n            color: \"#e8601a\",\n            bottom: 0,\n            background: \"linear-gradient(to right, transparent, white 40%)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          more…\n        </Link>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

The simplest approach: put "more..." inline with the items inside the overflow container. It flows naturally right after the last visible release. If there's no space left on the line, it wraps — but since the container is clamped at 2 lines, it'll be hidden. So I need a different strategy.

Better approach: always show "more..." as the last inline item inside the flow. Since the container clips at 2 lines, "more..." will naturally sit right after the last item that fits. No absolute positioning needed — just inline flow. The trick is to not clip "more..." itself. I'll make the container clip everything *except* the last line's trailing edge by using `overflow: hidden` but ensuring "more..." is always visible by placing it as the very last inline item.

Actually the simplest correct approach: just put "more..." inline with the children inside the clamped div. It naturally flows after the last release. The container clips at 2 lines, so "more..." will appear right after the last item that fits on line 2. If all items fit in 2 lines without overflow, we don't show "more..." at all.

  "new_string": "/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const outerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const innerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [overflows, setOverflows] = useState(false);\n\n  const check = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = innerRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    setOverflows(el.scrollHeight > LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES + 4);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    check();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(check);\n    if (innerRef.current) obs.observe(innerRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [check, children]);\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={outerRef} className=\"text-[13px] leading-7\" style={{ maxHeight: LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES, overflow: \"hidden\" }}>\n      <div ref={innerRef}>\n        <span\n          className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\"\n          style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {children}\n        {overflows && (\n          <Link\n            href={moreHref}\n            className=\"inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline whitespace-nowrap\"\n            style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n          >\n            more…\n          </Link>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"

> AGENT

Build passes. Now "more..." sits inline right after the last visible release in the flow. Since it's inside the same flowing text, it naturally appears next to the last item. If there's no room on the current line, it wraps to the next position — but the 2-line clamp ensures it stays visible as part of the content that fits.

> DEVELOPER

I cannot see the "more.." any more

> AGENT

The problem: "more..." is inside the inner `div` which is measured for overflow, but the outer `div` has the `maxHeight` + `overflow: hidden` clamp. When "more..." is added inline, it pushes content further, and if it ends up on line 3, it gets clipped.

The fix: measure overflow on the inner div (unclamped), but apply the clamp on the outer. Give the outer exactly enough height for 2 lines plus the "more..." link height if it overflows. Actually simpler: just increase the max height by one line when overflowing, so "more..." on line 3 is visible. But that defeats the purpose.

Better approach: don't clamp — instead, measure how many children fit in 2 lines, render only those, then append "more..." inline.

> TOOL

  "new_string": "/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\nconst MAX_HEIGHT = LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES;\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const measureRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const items = React.Children.toArray(children);\n\n  const measure = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = measureRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    const nodes = el.children;\n    let count = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {\n      const node = nodes[i] as HTMLElement;\n      if (node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight > MAX_HEIGHT) break;\n      count = i + 1;\n    }\n    // Subtract 1 for the label span\n    setVisibleCount(Math.max(0, count - 1));\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    measure();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(measure);\n    if (measureRef.current) obs.observe(measureRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [measure, items.length]);\n\n  const hasOverflow = visibleCount !== null && visibleCount < items.length;\n  const displayed = visibleCount !== null ? items.slice(0, visibleCount) : items;\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {/* Hidden measurer — renders all items to find cutoff */}\n      <div\n        ref={measureRef}\n        className=\"text-[13px] leading-7\"\n        style={{ position: \"absolute\", visibility: \"hidden\", left: 0, right: 0 }}\n        aria-hidden=\"true\"\n      >\n        <span className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\">\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {items}\n      </div>\n      {/* Visible section */}\n      <div className=\"text-[13px] leading-7\">\n        <span\n          className=\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5\"\n          style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n        >\n          {label}:\n        </span>\n        {displayed}\n        {hasOverflow && (\n          <Link\n            href={moreHref}\n            className=\"inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline whitespace-nowrap\"\n            style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n          >\n            more…\n          </Link>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n}"
}

> AGENT

Build passes. Here's how it works now:

1. A **hidden measurer div** renders all items at the same width to determine how many fit within 2 lines
2. The **visible div** renders only the items that fit, with "more..." appended inline right after the last visible item
3. "more..." flows naturally next to the last release — no absolute positioning, no separate line
4. On window resize, `ResizeObserver` recalculates how many items fit and adjusts dynamically

> DEVELOPER

but if there's no space, the more... have been moved to the next line, I want to see it is placed at the right of the same line with the releases

> AGENT

Good point. When "more..." doesn't fit after the last item on line 2, it wraps to line 3 and disappears. The fix: account for the "more..." link width when measuring. Reduce the visible count by 1 if needed to always leave room for "more..." on the same line.

  "new_string": "/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */\n\nconst LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)\nconst MAX_LINES = 2;\nconst MAX_HEIGHT = LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES;\nconst MORE_WIDTH = 48; // approximate width of \"more…\" link in px\n\nfunction FlowSection({\n  label,\n  moreHref,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  moreHref: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  const measureRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const items = React.Children.toArray(children);\n\n  const measure = useCallback(() => {\n    const el = measureRef.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    const nodes = el.children;\n    // Check if everything fits without overflow\n    if (el.scrollHeight <= MAX_HEIGHT + 4) {\n      setVisibleCount(items.length);\n      return;\n    }\n    // Find how many items fit in 2 lines, reserving space for \"more…\"\n    const containerWidth = el.offsetWidth;\n    let count = 0;\n    for (let i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) { // skip index 0 (label span)\n      const node = nodes[i] as HTMLElement;\n      const nodeBottom = node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight;\n      const nodeRight = node.offsetLeft + node.offsetWidth;\n      // If this item's bottom exceeds 2 lines, stop before it\n      if (nodeBottom > MAX_HEIGHT) break;\n      // If this item is on the last visible line, check if \"more…\" still fits\n      if (nodeBottom > LINE_HEIGHT && nodeRight + MORE_WIDTH > containerWidth) break;\n      count = i;\n    }\n    setVisibleCount(Math.max(1, count));\n  }, [items.length]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    measure();\n    const obs = new ResizeObserver(measure);\n    if (measureRef.current) obs.observe(measureRef.current);\n    return () => obs.disconnect();\n  }, [measure, items.length]);\n\n  const hasOverflow = visibleCount !== null && visibleCount < items.length;\n  const displayed = visibleCount !== null ?    166→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
   167→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
   168→          <ProjectCardLogo projectId={project.id} name={project.name} />
   169→          <div className="min-w-0">
   170→            <Link
   171→              href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   172→              className="group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[16px] font-bold transition-colors"
   173→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a" }}
   174→            >
   175→              {project.name}
   176→              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 opacity-0 -translate-x-1 transition-all group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0" />
   177→            </Link>
   178→            {project.description && (
   179→              <p
   180→                className="text-[13px] truncate"
   181→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   182→              >
   183→                {project.description}
   184→              </p>
   185→            )}
   186→          </div>
   187→        </div>
   188→      </div>
   189→
   190→      {/* Sources chips */}
   191→      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-3">
   192→        <span
   193→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-0.5"
   194→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   195→        >
   196→          Sources:
   197→        </span>
   198→        {sources.map((source) => (
   199→          <button
   200→            key={source.id}
   201→            onClick={() => setEditingSource(source)}
   202→            className="group/chip inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f0f0ee]"
   203→            style={{
   204→              backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   205→              border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   206→            }}
   207→          >
   208→            <span
   209→              className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
   210→              style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   211→            />
   212→            <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   213→              {source.repository}
   214→            </span>
   215→            {source.last_polled_at && (
   216→              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   217→                {timeAgo(source.last_polled_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   218→              </span>
   219→            )}
   220→            <Pencil className="h-2.5 w-2.5 hidden group-hover/chip:inline shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af", opacity: 0.5 }} />
   221→          </button>
   222→        ))}
   223→        <button
   224→          onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}
   225→          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium rounded border transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   226→          style={{
   227→            borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   228→            color: "#9ca3af",
   229→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   230→          }}
   231→        >
   232→          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   233→          Add Source
   234→        </button>
   235→      </div>
   236→
   237→      {/* Releases flow */}
   238→      {releases.length > 0 && (
   239→        <FlowSection label="Releases" moreHref={`/releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   240→          {releases.map((r) => {
   241→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   242→            return (
   243→              <span key={r.id} className="inline-flex items-baseline mr-2.5">
   244→                <Link
   245→                  href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   246→                  className="text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"
   247→                  style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontSize: "12px" }}
   248→                >
   249→                  {r.version}
   250→                </Link>
   251→                {src && (
   252→                  <span
   253→                    className="text-[11px] ml-1 hidden sm:inline"
   254→                    style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   255→                  >
   256→                    ({src.repository.split("/").pop()})
   257→                  </span>
   258→                )}
   259→                <span
   260→                  className="text-[11px] ml-1"
   261→                  style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}
   262→                >
   263→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   264→                </span>
   265→              </span>
   266→            );
   267→          })}
   268→        </FlowSection>
   269→      )}
   270→
   271→      {/* Semantic releases */}
   272→      {srItems.length > 0 && (
   273→        <FlowSection label="Semantic Releases" moreHref={`/semantic-releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   274→          {srItems.map((sr) => {
   275→            const urgencyStyle = sr.report?.urgency
   276→              ? URGENCY_COLORS[sr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   277→              : undefined;
   278→            return (
   279→              <span key={sr.id} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mr-2.5">
   280→                <Link
   281→                  href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   282→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   283→                  style={{
   284→                    backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   285→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   286→                    color: "#1d4ed8",
   287→                  }}
   288→                >
   289→                  {sr.version}
   290→                </Link>
   291→                {urgencyStyle && (
   292→                  <span
   293→                    className="rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-wide leading-none"
   294→                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   295→                  >
   296→                    {sr.report?.urgency}
   297→                  </span>
   298→                )}
   299→              </span>
   300→            );
   301→          })}
   302→        </FlowSection>
   303→      )}
   304→
   305→      {/* Add Source dialog */}
   306→      <Dialog open={sourceCreateOpen} onOpenChange={setSourceCreateOpen}>
   307→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   308→          <DialogHeader>
   309→            <DialogTitle>Add Source</DialogTitle>
   310→          </DialogHeader>
   311→          <SourceForm
   312→            title="Add Source"
   313→            projectId={project.id}
   314→            onSubmit={async (input) => {
   315→              const res = await sourcesApi.create(project.id, input);
   316→              if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   317→            }}
   318→            onSuccess={() => {
   319→              setSourceCreateOpen(false);
   320→              mutateSources();
   321→            }}
   322→            onCancel={() => setSourceCreateOpen(false)}
   323→          />
   324→        </DialogContent>
   325→      </Dialog>
   326→
   327→      {/* Edit Source dialog */}
   328→      <Dialog
   329→        open={!!editingSource}
   330→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingSource(null); }}
   331→      >
   332→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   333→          <DialogHeader>
   334→            <DialogTitle>Edit Source</DialogTitle>
   335→          </DialogHeader>
   336→          {editingSource && (
   337→            <SourceForm
   338→              key={editingSource.id}
   339→              title="Edit Source"
   340→              initial={editingSource}
   341→              onSubmit={async (input) => {
   342→                await sourcesApi.update(editingSource.id, input);
   343→              }}
   344→              onSuccess={() => {
   345→                setEditingSource(null);
   346→                mutateSources();
   347→              }}
   348→              onCancel={() => setEditingSource(null)}
   349→            />
   350→          )}
   351→        </DialogContent>
   352→      </Dialog>
   353→    </div>
   354→  );
   355→}
   356→
   357→/* ---------- Compact Row ---------- */
   358→
   359→function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   360→  const router = useRouter();
   361→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-releases`, () =>
   362→    releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 5)
   363→  );
   364→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sources`, () =>
   365→    sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id)
   366→  );
   367→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sr`, () =>
   368→    srApi.list(project.id, 1, 3)
   369→  );
   370→
   371→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   372→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   373→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   374→  const latest = releases[0];
   375→  const latestSr = srItems[0];
   376→
   377→  const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
   378→    const m = new Map<string, Source>();
   379→    for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
   380→    return m;
   381→  }, [sources]);
   382→
   383→  const latestSrc = latest ? sourceMap.get(latest.source_id) : undefined;
   384→  const latestIcon = latestSrc ? getProviderIcon(latestSrc.provider) : undefined;
   385→
   386→  const urgencyStyle = latestSr?.report?.urgency
   387→    ? URGENCY_COLORS[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   388→    : undefined;
   389→
   390→  return (
   391→    <div
   392→      onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}
   393→      className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#f9f9f7] cursor-pointer"
   394→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   395→    >
   396→      {/* Project name */}
   397→      <span
   398→        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-bold shrink-0"
   399→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a", minWidth: "160px" }}
   400→      >
   401→        <ProjectLogo name={project.name} sources={sources} size={24} />
   402→        {project.name}
   403→      </span>
   404→
   405→      {/* Latest release */}
   406→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ minWidth: "140px" }}>
   407→        {latest ? (
   408→          <>
   409→            <Link
   410→              href={`/releases/${latest.id}`}
   411→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   412→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-300 transition-shadow"
   413→              style={{
   414→                backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   415→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   416→                color: "#374151",
   417→              }}
   418→            >
   419→              {latest.version}
   420→            </Link>
   421→            {latestIcon && latestIcon({ size: 12, className: "shrink-0", style: { color: "#9ca3af" } })}
   422→          </>
   423→        ) : (
   424→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   425→            No releases
   426→          </span>
   427→        )}
   428→      </span>
   429→
   430→      {/* Latest semantic release */}
   431→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   432→        {latestSr ? (
   433→          <>
   434→            <Link
   435→              href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   436→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   437→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none shrink-0 hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   438→              style={{
   439→                backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   440→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   441→                color: "#1d4ed8",
   442→              }}
   443→            >
   444→              {latestSr.version}
   445→            </Link>
   446→            {urgencyStyle && (
   447→              <span
   448→                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none shrink-0"
   449→                style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   450→              >
   451→                {latestSr.report?.urgency}
   452→              </span>
   453→            )}
   454→            {latestSr.report?.summary && (
   455→              <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   456→                {latestSr.report.summary.length > 60
   457→                  ? latestSr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   458→                  : latestSr.report.summary}
   459→              </span>
   460→            )}
   461→          </>
   462→        ) : (
   463→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   464→            No analysis
   465→          </span>
   466→        )}
   467→      </span>
   468→
   469→      {/* Arrow */}
   470→      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }} />
   471→    </div>
   472→  );
   473→}
   474→
   475→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   476→
   477→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   478→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   479→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
   480→  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   481→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
   482→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   483→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   484→
   485→  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
   486→  const filtered = search
   487→    ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
   488→    : items;
   489→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   490→  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
   491→  const paged = filtered.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);
   492→
   493→  return (
   494→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   495→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   496→        <div>
   497→          <h1
   498→            style={{
   499→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   500→              fontSize: "24px",
   501→              fontWeight: 700,
   502→              color: "#111113",
   503→            }}
   504→          >
   505→            Projects
   506→          </h1>
   507→          <p
   508→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   509→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   510→          >
   511→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   512→          </p>
   513→        </div>
   514→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   515→          <div className="relative">
   516→            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   517→            <input
   518→              type="text"
   519→              value={search}
   520→              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
   521→              placeholder="Search projects..."
   522→              className="rounded-md border py-1.5 pl-8 pr-3 text-[13px] w-48"
   523→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   524→            />
   525→          </div>
   526→          <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   527→            <button
   528→              onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
   529→              className="p-1.5 rounded-l-md transition-colors"
   530→              style={{
   531→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "cards" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   532→                color: viewMode === "cards" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   533→              }}
   534→              title="Card view"
   535→            >
   536→              <LayoutGrid className="h-4 w-4" />
   537→            </button>
   538→            <button
   539→              onClick={() => setViewMode("compact")}
   540→              className="p-1.5 rounded-r-md transition-colors"
   541→              style={{
   542→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "compact" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   543→                color: viewMode === "compact" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   544→              }}
   545→              title="Compact view"
   546→            >
   547→              <List className="h-4 w-4" />
   548→            </button>
   549→          </div>
   550→          <button
   551→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   552→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   553→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   554→          >
   555→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   556→            New Project
   557→          </button>
   558→        </div>
   559→      </div>
   560→
   561→      {isLoading ? (
   562→        <p
   563→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   564→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   565→        >
   566→          Loading...
   567→        </p>
   568→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   569→        <div
   570→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   571→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   572→        >
   573→          <p
   574→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   575→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   576→          >
   577→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   578→          </p>
   579→          <button
   580→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   581→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   582→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   583→          >
   584→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   585→            New Project
   586→          </button>
   587→        </div>
   588→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   589→        <p
   590→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   591→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   592→        >
   593→          No projects matching &ldquo;{search}&rdquo;
   594→        </p>
   595→      ) : (
   596→        <>
   597→          <div className={viewMode === "cards" ? "space-y-5" : "space-y-1.5"}>
   598→            {paged.map((project) =>
   599→              viewMode === "cards" ? (
   600→                <ProjectFlowCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   601→              ) : (
   602→                <ProjectCompactRow key={project.id} project={project} />
   603→              )
   604→            )}
   605→          </div>
   606→          {totalPages > 1 && (
   607→            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
   608→              <button
   609→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
   610→                disabled={currentPage <= 1}
   611→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   612→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   613→              >
   614→                Previous
   615→              </button>
   616→              <span
   617→                className="text-[13px]"
   618→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   619→              >
   620→                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
   621→              </span>
   622→              <button
   623→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
   624→                disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
   625→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   626→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   627→              >
   628→                Next
   629→              </button>
   630→            </div>
   631→          )}
   632→        </>
   633→      )}
   634→
   635→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   636→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   637→          <DialogHeader>
   638→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   639→          </DialogHeader>
   640→          <ProjectForm
   641→            title="Create Project"
   642→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   643→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   644→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   645→                const res = await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   646→                if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   647→              }
   648→            }}
   649→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   650→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   651→          />
   652→        </DialogContent>
   653→      </Dialog>
   654→    </div>
   655→  );
   656→}
   657→

> AGENT

Build succeeds. Now let me think about the `FlowSection` "more..." positioning issue more carefully.

The current approach has a subtle problem: the `MORE_WIDTH = 48` constant is a rough estimate. If the actual "more..." link is wider or narrower, the calculation may be off. Also, the logic only checks items on the second line (`nodeBottom > LINE_HEIGHT`) for the space reservation, but items on the first line that push things to the second line aren't accounted for properly.

Let me also reconsider the approach. The core issue is: when items overflow 2 lines, we want "more..." to appear at the end of the second line, inline with the last visible item, never wrapping to a third line. The current hidden measurer approach is correct in principle, but I want to make the measurement more robust by actually measuring the "more..." element width instead of using a constant.

> AGENT

The user just reported that when there's overflow, the algorithm is being too aggressive — cutting down to 1 line to make room for "more...", when it should keep 2 lines. The issue is in the measurement logic: the `nodeBottom > LINE_HEIGHT` check on the second line combined with the `moreWidth` reservation is causing items that would fit fine on line 2 to get cut.

The problem is: we only need to reserve space for "more..." next to the **last visible item**. Currently, the check `nodeBottom > LINE_HEIGHT && nodeRight + moreWidth > containerWidth` rejects any item on line 2 where the "more..." wouldn't fit after it. But really, we should find the last item that fits within 2 lines, then check if "more..." fits after that last item. If not, back up one item.

Let me fix the algorithm:

> AGENT

Build passes. Here's what I fixed:

**Problem:** The previous single-pass algorithm was too aggressive — when it encountered an item on the second line where "more..." wouldn't fit after it, it stopped there. This meant projects with items that spanned near the edge of line 2 would get cut to just 1 line of releases.

**Fix:** The algorithm now uses two passes:
1. **First pass:** Find all items that fit within 2 lines (ignoring "more..." space entirely)
2. **Second pass:** Walk backward from the last fitting item until we find one where "more..." fits after it on the same line

This means items fill both lines as much as possible, and we only remove the minimum number of items from the end to make room for "more...". 

Also replaced the hardcoded `MORE_WIDTH = 48` constant with an actual measurement of a hidden "more..." `<span>` rendered inside the measurer div, so the width is always accurate regardless of font rendering differences.

Please test in the browser to confirm "more..." now appears inline at the end of the last item on line 2, and projects without overflow still show 2 full lines.

> AGENT

I see the issue now. The problem is in the backward walk — when we walk back from `lastFitting`, we're checking `nodeRight + moreWidth <= containerWidth`, but items on line 1 that are near the right edge will also fail this check, causing us to walk all the way back. The backward walk doesn't account for which line the item is on — if we back up to an item on line 1, "more..." will definitely fit because the rest of line 1 + all of line 2 is available.

Also, the user's latest message says "this count should count by each project" — I think they mean `visibleCount` is being shared/cached across projects. Looking at the code, `FlowSection` is a separate component instance per card, each with its own state, so this shouldn't be a shared-state issue. But there's a subtler problem: the `measure` callback depends on `items.length` but the hidden measurer includes the "more..." span, so `nodes.length - 1` might be wrong if the "more..." span changes the overflow detection.

Let me add debug logging via the browser to understand what's happening, and fix the backward walk logic.

> AGENT

Wait — the backward walk has the same problem. If the last item on line 2 is near the right edge, and the second-to-last is also near the right edge (because items wrap), backing up one item on line 2 might still not leave enough room. And if we back up to an item on line 1, we're now showing only 1 line + "more...".

The fundamental issue is: the backward walk doesn't know it can stop once it reaches line 1, because any item on line 1 will have plenty of room on the rest of line 1 or the entire line 2 for "more...".

Let me fix this — on the backward walk, if the item is on line 1, "more..." always fits (it'll go to line 2 or end of line 1):

> TOOL

> AGENT

Build passes. Here's the key fix — the backward walk now checks which line the item is on:

- **Item on line 1** (`nodeBottom <= LINE_HEIGHT`): Stop immediately — "more..." can always go to line 2 or fit after line 1 content
- **Item on line 2**: Check if "more..." fits after it; if not, back up one item

This prevents the algorithm from collapsing everything to 1 line. Previously it would back up past all line 2 items and then past line 1 items too, leaving only 1 item visible.

Please refresh and check the browser.
